##################
## ELDALI NAMES ##
##################

## This time 100% strict Quenya! Well... OK, 99% strict Quenya. :-)

##################
## QUENYA NOTES ##
##################

## FEMALE:
## -iel   - Upperclass names for rulers AND Titans.
## -is    - Common first names AND Battleships.
## -ien   - Science ships.
## -ven   - Colonizers.
## -ie    - Generic ships.
## -wen   - Ion cannons.
## -isse  - Gaias.
## -wende - Ocean.

## MALE:
## -ion - Upperclass names for rulers AND Corvettes.
## -on  - Common first names AND Cruisers.
## -ner - Research stations AND Observation Stations.
## -ano - Mining stations.
## -o   - Transports.

## GENDER-NEUTRAL NOTES: Common second names.

## WEAPONS: Defense platforms.

## HORSES: Constructors.
